[22/03/2020, 19:12:21] Liz: SUMMARY OF THE NATIONAL EMERGENCY RESPONSE COMMITTEE PRESS BRIEFING 

☑️ There is an additional 8 Corona virus cases in Kenya, bringing the total number to 15. None of them is in the ICU 
☑️ 363 persons who had come into contact with the 8 are being traced
☑️ Government continues to insist on high levels of hygiene and maintaining social distancing as an effective measure to avoid contracting the virus. 
☑️ Kenyans must adjust and change their lifestyles. Behaving normal under the circumstances is akin to having a death wish

MEASURES/DIRECTIVES
☑️ All international flights suspended effective Wednesday (25th March) at midnight apart from cargo flights. Everyone coming into the country between now and Wednesday to undergo quarantine in a government facility at their own expense 
☑️ All persons who violate quarantine regulations to be quarantined forcefully at their own expense and later charged. This will apply to a senior government official in Kilifi who failed to self- quarantine and has since tested positive. 
☑️ Effective midnight, all bars will remain closed until further notice. Restaurants to remain open but only for the purposes of take away services 
☑️ All PSVs must adhere to the directive issued on Friday (March 20th) failure to which respective Sacco licenses will be revoked 
☑️ Government has suspended all churches and mosques gatherings; funerals are restricted to family members (up to 15 people). Weddings not allowed. All forms of gatherings even within the streets not allowed
☑️ The National Government Administration Officers (NGAO)under the Ministry of Interior to enforce measures and directives issued. Moving forward displinary action will be taken towards those who will fail to comply 
☑️ The National Police Service to enforce the public health act, the outlined matatu regulations and quarantine. The police will also support NGAO in the enforcement of various outlined measures and directives.

[23/03/2020, 10:04:52] Ann M: Health Cabinet Secretary (CS) Mutahi Kagwe has confirmed that Kilifi County Deputy Governor Gideon Saburi has coronavirus disease.

The Health CS said those who came into contact with Mr. Saburi, who defied an order to self-quarantine, are now in self-isolation.

Mr. Saburi sparked anger after it emerged that he refused to self-quarantine as directed by the government after jetting back into the country from Germany.

Health workers fumigated the offices where Mr. Saburi is said to have held meetings with Kilifi County officials shortly after arriving from Germany.

Mr. Saburi is said to have traveled to Germany mid-February and jetted back March 6, 2020, via Amsterdam.

On March 7, the deputy governor met a group of Rabai MCAs at Royal Hotel in Mazeras for preparations of the International Women’s Day function in Rabai.

He had breakfast with the MCAs before proceeding for a burial of the late former Deputy Governor Kenneth Kamto’s bodyguard.

On his trip to the burial, Mr. Saburi was accompanied by Rabai MP William Kamoti and afterward, they returned to Royal Hotel in Mazeras.

He is said to have attended three other burials and two weddings in between.

On March 18, he is said to have accompanied his driver to a place called Karama Mbuzi in Majengo area of Mombasa where they had a sumptuous meal of Ugali and mshkaki (roast meat on a stick).

The following day on March 19, he was spotted in a night club in Bamburi where he mingled with a number of revelers.

On Friday of March 20, again, alongside his driver they took refreshments at Cafesserie-Mombasa within Nyali center where he also mingled with a number of revelers for several hours before he left for his house in Bamburi.

It was on this day that Health Cabinet Secretary Mutahi Kagwe announced that the government is tracking a suspected patient in Kilifi who happened to be the DG. 

Sources said he was approached by health officials where his samples were taken and advised to self-quarantine but he declined.

On watching the CS’s live address, he reportedly left his home in Bamburi secretly and went to a house in Mtwapa where he reportedly went hid.

However, Kilifi Governor Amason Kingi reportedly dispatched county askaris, leading to a dramatic arrest as Mr. Saburi resisted.

The county askaris took him to Coast Provincial General Hospital in Mombasa where he is said to have been hospitalised in an isolation center.

[03/01/2021, 15:31:54] Liz: *COMPANIES BENEFICIAL OWNERSHIP INFORMATION REGULATIONS, 2019*
I hope that you are keeping well and safe.
The Companies Registry has introduced a new mandatory requirement for all companies with effect from 13th October 2020 to keep and maintain a register for all beneficial owners of the company, which is stipulated in Section 93 of the Companies Act 2015. The Companies (Beneficial Ownership Information) Regulations, 2019 provides guidelines on how to maintain the register for beneficial owners. The Companies Act 2015 defines a beneficial owner as: “A natural person who ultimately owns or controls a legal person or arrangements or the natural person on whose behalf a transaction is conducted, and includes those persons who exercise ultimate effective control over a legal person or arrangement”.

*Regulations*
The Regulations set out in Companies (Beneficial Ownership Information) Regulations, 2019 apply to beneficial owners who:
1. hold at least 10% of the issued shares of the company either directly or indirectly;
2. exercises at least 10% of the voting rights in the company
3. hold a right to directly or indirectly appoint or remove a director of the company; or
4. exercises significant influence or control over the company (whether directly or indirectly)
The Register of Members with respect to beneficial owners shall include the following particulars: the full name; national identity card number/passport number; personal identification number; nationality; date of birth; postal address; residential address; current telephone number; current email address; occupation; date on which any person became a beneficial owner; date on which any person ceased to be a beneficial owner; nature of ownership or control and any other detail the Registrar may from time to time require.
*Timelines*
Companies are required to within 30 days of preparation of the register lodge a copy of the register with the Registrar. Any amendments to the register shall be lodged with the Registrar within 14 days with the exception of listed companies.
*Non-compliance penalties*
This is a MANDATORY requirement. Failure to comply with the disclosure of beneficial owners and filing requirements is an offence under the Companies Act that on conviction attracts a penalty on each officer of up to KES 500,000 (approx. USD 4,800) and a further KES 50,000 for continued non-compliance for each offence.

*Required*
There will be need prepare the registers of the BO’s Directors/Shareholders as per the Resolution and file with registrar of companies.

[17/05/2021, 12:53:06] Liz: Feedback
Successes/ Winning strategy from well perfoming sites e.g. Saba Saba station
•	Dealer Presence at all times or well presented by competent manager
•	Optimum stocking – 3 days stock at any given time
•	Housekeeping – site very clean at all times
•	8 step service – observe that all csa do it. Ensure that customer feels important
•	Remuneration- proper pay for CSA as per wage bill, being available for them
•	Dealer initiative – discounts from own margin
Lubes successes
•	CSA engagement with customers
•	Product available – not just in stock but also available on forecourt. 
•	Also can do own collection from depot
•	Incentive to CSA – have targets and look to other areas to sell/ get their own orders
To note:
•	Stock – place orders in good time bearing in mind delivery time for lube and LPG especially
•	Housekeeping
•	Incident reporting
•	Safety meetings by CSA
•	Proper PPE for all site staff – safety shoes etc
•	Offloading procedure followed and a specific person/marshall to do it
•	Ocard system and loyalty program
•	Wireless automation – all sites connected by end of month 
•	By next month we will have new contractors for maintenance for pumps, air gauges etc
•	Working capital – consider bank guarantee, dealer financing for 7 days e.g. Stanbic,

[04/08/2021, 14:45:45] Liz: Good evening team. The Kilifi County Covid-19 Emergency and Response Committee held a meeting today, Thursday, 29/07/2021at Kilifi County Governor's Iffice Lawns starting from 2.00 p.m to 5.20 p.m.
The meeting was convened in response to the steadily rising cases of Covid-19 disease in Kilifi County, to deliberate on areas of achievements and propose strategies to stop the spread. The meeting was chaired by Kilifi Governor, Hon. Amason Kingi and Co-Chaired by Kilifi County Commissioner, Mr. Kutswa Olaka

OBSERVATIONS
1. Funerals/Weddings  are attracting huge crowds and politicians in total disregard of social distancing
2. Politicians have slowly started holding public political gatherings.
3. Availability of medical oxygen has gone down.
4. Congestion of people is being witnessed at      
            - Open market areas
            -  Burials
            -  Schools
            - Matatu transport sector
5. Physical sporting activities taking place.

RESOLUTIONS
1. Hospitals - strictly no visitation
2. Government Services - scale down on non essential services and encourage online services.
3. Open markets-suspended for 30 days. County government to issues notice immediately.
4. Regular Markets- Market committees and County Enforcement department to ensure compliance with Covid-19 protocols
5. Bars - to close by 7.00 p.m., proprietors who don't comply be arrested forthwith and licences revoked by County Government . 
6. Church/ Mosque - Strictly implement the 1/3 rule and the number of worshipers be displayed conspicuously at church entrance
-No congregants without masks
-Church service to take a maximum of one and a half hours.
 - Presiding Priests/Imams whose places of worship flout MOH protocols be held accountable.
7.  Public Transport - vehicle to fully comply with MOH protocols rule.
   - Multiagency enforcement team to resume activities on the roads immediately starting on Friday, 30/07/2021. 
   - Driver and conductor whose vehicles flout rules to be arrested and sacco licences be withdrawn by NTSA.
 - All PSV vehicles by Friday, 06/08/2021 to have been inspected at Subcounties and acquired Certification on Covid-19 compliance from County Commissioner
8. Burials/Weddings -  In Kilifi County bereaved families to bury their deceased relatives latest within 48 hours after confirmation of a death. This will be observed in the next 30 days.
 - Strictly no overnight vigils
-No disco matanga s
-Maintain maximum of 100 mourners/wedding guests
-Burial ceremony to take a maximum of 2 hours.
-  Mortuary management to release bodies not later than 12 noon to facilitate burial on the same day.
9. Commitment template be in place for burials and weddings - The County Commissioner's Office to develop a template indicating all the Covid-19 protocols on Burials/Weddings for family members/Organizers to sign.
10. Imams/ Pastors - should decline to preside over burials/ weddings that contravene Covid-19 protocols.
11. Physical Contact Sports - All tournaments suspended forthwith for the next 30 days
     -  County Director of Education to write to all the Headteachers/Principals regarding suspension of sporting activities in all schools grounds.
12. Political Gatherings - They remain banned as per the National Covid-19 protocols.
13. Keep a record of worshipers in all churches and mosques for the sake of contact tracing
14. Boda Boda and Tuktuks - Enforcement on Covid-19 to be upscaled 
15. Public Beaches -  Currently they do not pose a  major threat by must be kept under watch and necessary action taken Incase of any breach.

[07/11/2022, 20:03:59] Ann M: Good Afternoon Madam, hope this finds you well. I write to you to pray for a second chance at your establishment at Voi bar and restaurant. I have been thinking hard to figure out how could it be that there be a cash flow from bar to the kitchen and yet the kitchen still have stock issues. I have done a lot of research to understand why such a trend could occur and how to avoid it and I have discovered several factors that cause this.

1. Working capital is directly proportional to sales. Ie if  I use working capital of ksh 30,000 to make a sale of 150 k monthly , if the sale increase to 250 k, that about 60% increase, then the working capital should be increased at the same percentage . 

2 . Working capital is time bound, ie if I take out working  capital from the business for few days let's say two days, then I should not expect to get any margin for those two days, and if I get that margin, then that money comes from other sources ( borrowed) and one is creating unreplaceable gap from that source in this case the bar.

3. The working capital has what I would call the residual Amount which should never be counted as working capital, ie if I buy 10 kgs of goat as starting stock then I always order new stock when I have a balance stock of 2kg then I should never calculate my working capital as 10 kgs but 8 kgs instead but also that residual should always be there .

4. Any new product introduced for sale should never be bought by existing working capital monies unless is product substitution.

5. Every working capital has its specific optimum sales,ie 50 k working capital has a different optimum from 100 k working capital. 

I have also noted that this problems are only associated with increasing sales scenario, but in case of decreasing sales or static sales, this problem is never experienced because one would always have enough stock Incase of static sales or will always have excess stock Incase of decreasing sales. 

Now I believe what happened has taught me a lot on how to handle this situation better. 

Given a chance, I believe I would do better now and avoid such mistakes going forward.

Thank you for bearing with my long text. And I hope you shall find my prayers worthy for your consideration

Regards:

Mwaniki.

[14/06/2023, 15:55:42] Liz: Malicious injury to property of the Kilifi Boatyard

 This morning 14th June, at 8.00  the Deputy Head of Security of Kilifi Plantation instructed a group of people to dig up the KIMAWASCO pipe line that runs through the property next to the Kilifi Boatyard and supplies the water to the Boatyard. This property belongs to an entity controlled by Mr Chris Wilson. The Deputy Head, by his words, acted on instructions of Mr Chris Wilson.

His instructions were to dig up the water meter and the pipe line that supplies Kilifi Boatyard. He acted accordingly and the line was dug up.
The pipeline and the meter are the property of KIMAWASCO.
The Kenya Police Service, the DCIO and his team and the KIMAWASCO Area Manager were called to the scene.
The Police asked to see any legal document authorising Kilifi Plantation to act in this way. None was forthcoming.

The Area Manager of KIMAWASCO stated that Kilifi Plantation was guilty of malicious damage to property of KIMAWASCO and to its provision of its water to Kilifi Boatyard.

As the individuals involved obviously acted on instructions from above, Kilifi Boatyard and KIMAWASCO agreed that the individuals should not,  at this  time , bear the responsibility for this action. They agreed with the Kenya Police that no charges would be filed against these individuals, provided the water supply by Kimawasco to the Boatyard was  reinstated immediately and that the costs of this reconnection should be born by Kilifi Plantations.

Concurrently

Another group of people under command of the Kilifi Plantation Security Team entered the Boatyard this morning and started to break down the major office building. They were stopped as they had no warrant.

Limited water available at the Boatyard for the time being.

I regret to ask all visitors to the Boatyard to be indulgent with the limited supply of water for the time being, until a full KIMAWASO supply has been restored.
[15/06/2023, 10:59:37] Liz: Hi

As I promised a reply, I will keep it as brief  and as relevant as possible. Of course, the reliance of unchecked and unverified social media posts is in itself unwise. 

*Fact:* KPL is in current possession of a TOL (Temporary OCUPANCY  LICENCE) of 6 acres in the Boatyard area issued by the NLC. 

*Fact:* KBY/Bateman applied to and was given, by KFS, a Special USE license resulting in what would be a double allocation, if the SUL is to be taken at face value and Bateman is to be believed on its import, which is not legal. This double allocation is very odd and raises questions as to how KBY/ Bateman obtained this document without the involvement of the holder of the TOL. Furthermore, the interpretation by Bateman/ KBY of this document (SUL) is for all intents and purposes, inaccurate. This is a matter now in court, and KPL will not speak anymore on the issue until the matter is settled in that forum.

*Fact:* KBY/Bateman, after obtaining the SUL from KFS, repudiated the Lease they had on the land with us which allowed them to use of facilities situated on Pylon land as well as road access through properties owned or affiliated to KPL. Effectively KBY/Bateman chose to cancel the relationship they had with KPL.

*Fact:* Consequent to this action by KBY/Bateman, KPL requested KBY to immediately remove their water tanks and other installations, including the Boatyard offices, from KPL land before expiry of the notice period. 
 
KBY/Bateman rushed off to call the DCI and KIMAWASCO who after being explained the above facts left without taking any further action against KPL or its staff. The pipes that were  lifted  are available for collection by KIMAWASCO officers at any time. 

*Fact:* A private owner of a property is within their rights to take whatever measures allowed in law to protect their interests, and in the context of KBY/Bateman, the fate of the KBY offices (which have not been brought down contrary to the sensational reports) and the access road to the boatyard which goes through private land not owned by KBY/Bateman will be considered and any action taken will be taken strictly within the law and following procedure and not on social media. 

*Fact:* Suffice it to say, KBY/ Bateman is engaged in an ill advised attempt at a classic land grab having been allowed to run a business, as a tenant, on land not owned by him.

Finally, it may be lost on some of you but these trees that are at the creek are only here because KP has protected them for more than a century now at significant cost to itself and will hopefully continue to do so long after we have have all left the scene.
